const motionOff = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
const olderBridge=mobileBridge, olderRhythm=mobileRhythm,olderScene=drawScene,olderBoss=drawBossClock;
function loaded(){return art.complete && art.naturalWidth>0;}
function styleLanding(){
  if(!loaded())return;
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--cinematic-art', `url("${art.src}")`);
  const overlay='linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(8,18,29,.97) 0%,rgba(9,26,39,.87) 46%,rgba(12,30,42,.29) 100%)';
  for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('.hero,.story-hero')) {
    el.style.backgroundImage=`${overlay},url("${art.src}")`;
    el.style.backgroundPosition='center,65% 37%';
    el.style.backgroundSize='cover,cover';
    el.style.borderColor='#e5c58f';
  }
}
art.onload=()=>{styleLanding()};
function rect(c,x,y,w,h,fill){c.fillStyle=fill;c.fillRect(x,y,w,h)}
function round(c,x,y,w,h,r,fill,stroke,lw=1){c.beginPath();c.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r);c.fillStyle=fill;c.fill();if(stroke){c.strokeStyle=stroke;c.lineWidth=lw;c.stroke()}}
function disc(c,x,y,r,fill,stroke,lw=1){c.beginPath();c.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);c.fillStyle=fill;c.fill();if(stroke){c.strokeStyle=stroke;c.lineWidth=lw;c.stroke()}}
function txt(c,s,x,y,sz=16,color='#fff',align='left',weight=800){c.save();c.font=`${weight} ${sz}px system-ui,'Noto Sans JP',sans-serif`;c.textAlign=align;c.textBaseline='middle';c.shadowColor='#02111c';c.shadowBlur=5;c.fillStyle=color;c.fillText(s,x,y);c.restore()}
function bloom(c,x,y,r,color,opacity=.22){const g=c.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,color);g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');c.save();c.globalAlpha=opacity;rect(c,x-r,y-r,2*r,2*r,g);c.restore()}
function setup(c){const W=c.canvas.width,H=c.canvas.height;c.save();c.setTransform(W/560,0,0,H/470,0,0);
  // Cropped to show the arch, warm sunset and genuinely detailed clockwork architecture.
  c.drawImage(art,0,330,780,654,0,0,560,470);
  const veil=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,560,0);veil.addColorStop(0,'rgba(2,17,29,.26)');veil.addColorStop(.42,'rgba(9,23,32,.16)');veil.addColorStop(1,'rgba(5,18,29,.22)');rect(c,0,0,560,470,veil);
  const fade=c.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,470);fade.addColorStop(0,'rgba(3,13,23,.35)');fade.addColorStop(.22,'transparent');fade.addColorStop(.74,'transparent');fade.addColorStop(1,'rgba(3,10,18,.90)');rect(c,0,0,560,470,fade);
}
function head(c,category,title){
 round(c,18,17,524,70,13,'rgba(8,22,33,.83)','rgba(255,222,165,.66)',1.5);
 rect(c,33,32,4,38,'#f5c883');
 txt(c,category,52,37,12,'#ecc994');txt(c,title,52,65,24,'#fff5e6');
}
function foot(c,primary,secondary,success=false){
 round(c,18,392,524,61,12,'rgba(5,22,34,.89)',success?'#9bffe1':'rgba(255,223,168,.68)',1.5);
 txt(c,primary,34,411,18,success?'#b9ffe9':'#ffe6b3');
 txt(c,secondary,34,436,14,'#e5f5ff');
